CABINET SECRETARIAT 
(Department of Personnel and Administrate Reforms) 

New Delhi, the 26th September, 1973 

G.S.R. 2488. — 'In exercise of the powers conferred by tho proviso o^ article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby make* 
the following rules regulating tho method of recruitment to the post of Library Attendant, in the Lat Bahadur Shastri National 
Academy of Administration, Mussoorie, namely 

1 . Short title and commencement.— (1) These rules may be called the Lai Bahadur Shastri National Academy of Administration 

(Library Attendant) Recruitment Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall conic into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of the post, its classification and the scale of pay .—The number of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay 
attached thereto shall be as specified in columns 3 to 5 of the Schedule annexed to these rules, 

3. Method of recruitment, age-limit, qualifications, etc .—The mothod of recruitment, age-limit, qualifications and other matter* 
relating to the said post shall bo as specified in columns 6 to 14 of the said schedule. 

4. Disqualifications : — 

No person 

(a) Who has entered indo or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) Who, having a spouse living, has entered into, or contracted a marriage with any person, shall be eligible for appointment to 
the said post. 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law 
applicable to such person and the other party to the marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the 
operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax .—Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may by order, for 
reasons to be recorded in writing relax any of the provision of these rule in respect of any class or category of persons. 

6. Saving .—Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations and other concession required to be provided for the Scheduled Castes, 
the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by tne Contral Government from timo 
to time in this regard/Scheme.

Sec. 3(i)] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA: OCTOBER It, 1975/ASVINA 19, 1897 


New Delhi, the 27th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2489. — 'In, exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, and in supersession of the 
Central Bureau of Investigation (Ovoseer and Sectional Officer) Recruitment Rules, 1969, the President hereby makes the following rules 
relating the method of recruitment to the post of Junior Engineer in the Central Bureau of Investigation, namely : — 

1 . Short title and commencement : — • 

(1) These rules may be called the Central Bureau of Investigation (Junior Engineer) Recruitment Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come, into force on the date of their Publication in the Official Gazotte. 

2. Number of posts, classification and scale of pay : — The numbor of posts, Its classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule hereto annexed. 

3 . Method of recruitment, ago limit and other qualifications The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and othermatters 
relating to the said post shall be as specified in column* 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 

4. Disqualifications 
No person, — 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, 
shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to su ch 
person and the other party to the marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this 
rule. 

5. Power to relax : — Whore the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may by order, 
for reason, to bo recorded in writing, relax any of the provision; of these rules with respect to any class or category of persons. 

6. Saving : — Nothing in the;e rules, shall affect researvations and other concessions required to be provided for the Scheduled 
Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government from 
time to time in this regard.

[Part II — 


GAR. 2490. — In pursuance of sub- rule (9) of rule 13 of 
the Central Secretariat Service Rules, 1962, the Central Gov- 
ernment In the Department of Personnel and Administrative 
Reforms in consultation with the Union Public Service Com- 
mission, hereby makes the following regulations, further to 
amend the Central Secretariat Service Section Officers’ Grade 
(Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1966, namely : — 

1. (1) These regulations may be called the Central Secre- 

tariat Service Section Officers’ Grade (Competitive 
Examination) Third Amendment Regulations, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Central Secretariat Service Section Officers’ Grade 
(Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1966 :— 

(i) regulation 5 shall be omitted. 

(ii) for regulation 9, the following regulation shall be 

substituted, namely : — 

“9. Penalty for misconduct : — A candidate who is or 
has been declared by the Commission to be 
guilty of : — 

(a) obtaining support for his candidature by any 

means, or 

(b) impersonating, or 

(c) procuring impersonation by any person, or 

(d) submitting fabricated documents or documents 

which have been tampered with, or 

(e) making statements which are incorrect or false. 

or suppressing mateflal information, or 

(f) resorting to any other irregular or improper 

means in connection with his candidature for 
the examination, or 

(g) using unfair means in the examination hall, or 

(h) misbehaving in the examination hall, or 

(i) attempting to commit or, as the case may be, 

abetting the commission of all or any of the 
acts specified in the foregoing clauses, may. 
in addition to rendering himself liable to cri- 
minal prosecution, be liable, — 

(A) to be disqualified by the Commission from 
the examination for which he is a candidate, 
or 

(B) to be debarred either permanently or for a 
specified period — 

(i) by the Commission, from any examination or 
selection held by them ; 

(ii) by the Central Government from any 
employment under them ; and 

(C) if he is already in service under the Central 
Government, to disciplinary action under the 
appropriate rules.” 

(No. 11/2/7 5-CS-II(i>]

G.S.R. 2492.— In pursuance of sub-rule (9) of rule 13 of 
the Central Secretariat Service Rules, 1962, the Central 
Government in the Department of Personnel and Administra- 
tive Reforms in consultation with the Union Public Service 
Commission, hereby makes the following regulations, further 
to amend the Central Secretariat Service Assistants' Grade 
(Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1965, namely : — 

1. (1) These regulations may be called the Central Secre- 

tariat Service Assistants' Grade (Competitive Exa- 
mination) Second Amendment Regulations, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication In the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Central Secretariat Service Assistants' Grade 
(Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1965 : — 

(i) regulation 5 shall be omitted. 

(ii) for regulation 9, the following regulation shall be 
substituted, namely : — 

“9. Penalty for misconduct A candidate who is or 
has been declared by the Commission to be guilty 
of : — 

(a) obtaining support for his candidature by any 

means, or 

(b) impersonating, or 

(c) procuring impersonation by any person, or 

(d) submitting fabricated documents or documents 

which have been tampered with, or ' 

(e) making 'statements which are incorrect or false, 

or suppressing material information, or 

(f) resorting to any other irregular or improper 

means in connection with his candidature for 
the examination, or 

(g) using unfair means in the examination hall, or 


(h) misbehaving in the examination hall, or 

(i) attempting to commit, or, as the case may be 

abetting the commission of all or any of the 
acts specified in the foregoing clauses, may, in 
addition to rendering himself liable to criminal 
prosecution, be liable — 

(A) to be disqualified by the Commission from 
the examination for which he is a candidate, 
or 

(B) to be debarred either permanently or for a 
specified period — 

(i) by the Commission, from any examination 

or selection held by them ; 

(ii) by the Central Government from any 

employment under them ; and 

(C) if he is already in service under Central Gov- 
ernment to disciplinary action under the 
appropriate rules.”

G.S.R. 2494, — In pursuance of " sub-paragraph (2) Of para- 
raph 2 of the Fifth Schedule to the Central Secretariat 
tenographers Service Rules, 1969, the Central Government 
in the Department of Personnel and Administrative Reforms 
in consultation with the Union Public Service Commission 
hereby makes the following regulations further to amend the 
Central Secretariat Stenographers Service (Grade II Limited 
Departmental Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1971, 
namely : — 

1. (1) These regulations may he called the Central Secre- 

tariat Stenographers Service (Grade II l imited 
Departmental Competitive Examination) Amend- 
ment Regulations, 1975, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Centra] Secretariat Stenographers Service (Grade 
IT Limited Departmental Competitive Examination) Regula- 
tions, 1971 — 

(i) regulation J shall be omitted, 

(ii) for regulation 9, the following regulation shall be 
substituted, namely : — 

"9. Penalty for misconduct : — A candidate who is or 
has been declared by the Institute of Secretariat 
Training and Management to be guilty of : — 

(a) obtaining support for his candidature bv any 

means, or 

(b) impersonating, or 

(c) procuring impersonation by any person, or 

(d) submitting fabricated documents or documents 

which have been tampered with, or 

(e) making statements which are Incorrect or false, 

or suppressing material information, or 

(f) resorting to any other irregular or improper 

means in connection w'ith his candidature for 
the examination, or 

(g) using unfair means in the examination hall, or 

(h) misbehaving in the examination hall, or 

(i) attempting to commit or, as the case may be, 

abetting the Commission of all or any of the 
acts specified in the foregoing clauses, may, in 
addition to rendering himself liable to criminal 
prosecution, be liable — 

(A) to be disqualified by the Institute of Secre- 
tariat Training and Management from (he 
examination for which he is a candidate, or 

(B) to be debarred either permanently or for a 
specified period — 

(i) by the Institute of Secretariat Training and 

Management from any examination or 
selection held by them ; 

(ii) by the Central Government from any 

employment under them ; and 

(C) to disciplinary action under the appropriate 
rules”,

G.S.R. 2493. — In pursuance of sub-paragraph (2) of para- 
graph 3 of the Sixth Schedule to the Central Secretariat 
Stenographers Service Rules, 1969, the Central Government 
in the Department of Personnel and Administrative Reforms 
in consultation with the Union Public Service Commission 
hereby makes the following regulations, further to amend the 
Central Secretariat Stenographers Service, Grade I (Limited 
Departmental Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1974, 
namely : — 

1. (1) These regulations may be called the Central Secre- 
tariat Stenographers Service, Grade I (Limited De- 
partmental Competitive Examination) Amendment 
Regulations, 1975. 

(2) They shall, come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 



Sec. 3 (i) ] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA: OCTOBER 11, 1975/ASVINA 19, 1897 2811 


2. In the Central Secretariat Stenographers Service, 
Grade I (Limited Department Competitive Examination), 
Regulations, 1974; 

(i) regulation 5 shall be omitted. 

(ii) for regulation 9, the following regulation shall be 
substituted, namely — 

''9. Penalty for misconduct : — A candidate who is or 
has been declared by the Commission to be 
guilty of 

(a) obtaining support for his candidature by any 

means, or 

(b) impersonating, or 

(c) procuring impersonation by any person, or 

(d) submitting fabricated documents or documents 

which have been tampered with, or 

(e) making statements which arc incorrect or false, 

or suppressing material information, or 

(f) resorting to any other irregular or improper 

means in connection with his candidature for 
the examination, or 

(g) using unfair means in the examination hall, oi 

(h) misbehaving in the examination hall, or 

(i) attempting to commit or, as the case may be, 

abetting the Commission of all or any of the 
acts specified in the foregoing clauses, may, in 
addition to rendering himself liable to criminal 
prosecution, be liable — 

(A) to be disqualified by the Commission from 
the examination for which he is a candidate, 
or 

(B) to be debarred either permanently or for a 
specified period — 

(i) by the Commission, from any examination 
or selection held by them ; 

(ii) by the Central Government from any em- 
ployment under them ; and 

(C) to disciplinary action under the appropriate 
rules”.

the Central Secretariat Stenographers Service Rules, 1969, the 
Central Government in the Department of Personnel and 
Administrative Reforms, hereby makes the following regula-

1969, namely : —

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official’ Gazette-

(i) regulation 5 shall be omitted.

G.S.R. 2497. — In pursuance of sub-rule (4) of rule 14 of 
the Central Secretariat Stenographers Service Rules, 1969, the 
Central Government in the Department of Personnel and 
Administrative Reforms, hereby makes the following regu- 
lations further to amend the Central Secretariat Stenographers 
Service (Grade III— Special Competitive Examination) Regu- 
lations, 1971, namely: — 

1. (1) These regulations may be called the Central Secre- 

tariat Stenographers Service (Grade III — Special 
Competitive Examination) Amendment Regulations, 
1975. 

(2J They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2, In the Central Secretariat Stenographers Service 
(Grade III. — Special Competitive Examination) Regulations, 
1971 

(i) regulation 5 shall be omitted. 

(ii) for regulation 9, the following regulation shall be 

substituted, namely :■ — 

"9. Penalty for misconduct A candidate who is or, 
has been declared by the Institute of Secretariat 
Training and Management to be guilty of; — 

(a) obtaining support for his candidature by any 

means, or 

(b) impersonating, or 

(c) procuring impersonation by any person, or 

(d) submitting fabricated documents or documents 

which have been tampered with, or 

(e) making statements which are incorrect or false, 

or suppressing material information, or 

(f) resorting to any other irregular or improper 

means in connection with his candidature for 
the examination, or 

(g) using unfair means in the examination hall, or 

(h) misbehaving in the examination hall, or 

(i) attempting to commit or, as the case may be, 

abetting the Commission of all or any of the 
acts specified in the foregoing clauses, may, in 
addition to rendering himself liable to criminal 
prosecution, be liable —

(A) to be disqualified by the Institute of Secre- 
tariat Training and Management from the 
examination for which he is a candidate, or 

(B) to be debarred either permanently or for a 
specified period — 

(i) by the Institute of Secretariat Training and 

Management from any examination or 
selection held by them ; 

(ii) by the Central Government from any 

employment under them ; and 

(C) if he is already in service under Central 
Government to disciplinary action under the 
appropriate rules."

means, or 

(b) impersonating, or

which have been tampered with, or

means in connection with his candidature for

acts specified in the foregoing clauses, may, in

(A) to be disqualified by the Institute of Secret 
Uniat Training and Management from the

specified period —

the Central Secretariat Clerical Service Rules, 1962 the

namely : —

publication in the Official Gazette.

Examination) Regulations, 1965 : —

substituted, namely 

" 9 . Penalty for misconduct A candidate who is or 
has been declared the institute of Secretariat 
Training and Management to be guilty of : — 

(a) obtaining support for his candidature by any 

means, or 

(b) impersonating, or 

(c) procuring impersonation by any person, or 

(d) submitting fabricated documents or documents 

which have been tampered with, or 

(c) making statements which are incorrect or false, 
or suppressing material information, or 

(f) resorting to any other irregular or improper 

means in connection with his candidature for 
the examination, or 

(g) using unfair means in the examination hall, or 

<h) misbehaving in the examination hall, or 

(i) attempting to commit or, a? the case may be, 
abetting the commission of all or any of the 
acts specified in the foregoing clauses, may, in 
addition to rendering himself liable to criminal 
prosecution, be liable — 

(A) to be disoualified by the Institute of Secre- 
tariat Training and Management, from the

specified period — 

(i) by the Institute of Secretariat Training and 

Management from any examination or 
selection held by them ; 

(ii) by the Central Government from any 
employment under them ; and 

(C) if he is already in service under Central 
Govermcnt to disciplinary action under the 
appropriate rules."

G.S.R. ,2500.— -Id pursuance of sub-rule (4) of rule 12, of 
the Central Secretariat Clerical Service Rules, 1962, the 
Central Government in the Department of Personnel and 
Administrative Reforms, hereby makes the following regu- 
lations further to amend the Central Secretariat Clerical 
Service (Lower Division Grade Competitive Examination for 
Class TV, Staff) Regulations, 1 969, namely ; — 

1. (1) These regulations may be called the Central Secre- 

tariat Clerical Service (Lower Division Competitive 
Examination for Class IV Staff) Amendment Regu- 
lations, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Central Secretariat Clerical Service (Lower Divi- 
sion Grade Competitive Examination for Class IV Staff) Regu- 
lations, 1969 : — 

(i) regulation 5 shall be omitted. 

(ii) for regulation 9, the following regulation shall be 
substituted, namely : — 

“9. Penalty for misconduct A candidate who is or 
has been declared by the Institute of Secretariat 
Training and Management to be guilty of: — 

fa) obtaining support for his candidature by any 
means, or 

(b) impersonating, 01 

(c) procuring impersonation by any person, or 

(d) submitting fabricated documents or documents 

which have been tampered with, or 

(e) making statements which are incorrect or false, 

or suppressing material information, or 

(f) resorting to any othpr irregular or improper 

connection with his candidature for the exami- 
nation, or 

(g) using unfair means in the examination hall, or 

(h) misbehaving in the examination hall, or 

(i) attempting to commit or, as the case may be, 

abetting the commission of all or any of the 
acts specified in the foregoing clauses, may, In 
addition to rendering himself liable to criminal 
prosecution, be liable — 

(A) to be disqualified by the Institute of Secre- 
tariat Training and Management from the 
examination for which he is a candidate, or 

(B) to be debarred either permanently or for a 
specified period — 

(i) by the Institute of Secretariat Training and 

Management from any examination or 
selection held by them ; 

(ii) by the Central Government from any 
employment under them ; and 

(C) to disciplinary action under the appropriate 
rules."

New Delhi, Ihe 29th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2502 .— In cxeicise of the powers conferred by Sub- 
section (1) of section 3 of the All India Services Act, 1951 
(61 of 1951), read with rule 7 of the Indian Forest Service 
(Recruitment) Rules, 1966, the Central Government in con- 
sultation with the Government, of the States concerned and 
the Union Public Service Commission, hereby makes the 
following regulations further to amend the Indian Forest 
Service (Amendment by Competitive Examination) Regula- 
tions, 1967, namely: — 

1. (1) These regulations may be called Ihe Indian Forest 

Service (Appointment by Competitive Examination) 
Second Amendment Regulations, 1975. 

(2) They shall be deemed to have come into force with 
ellect from the 6th October, 1973. 

2. In regulation 10 of the Indian Forest Service (Appoint- 
ment by Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1967. sub- 
regulation (2) shall be omitted. 

Explanatory Memorandum : 

Sub-rule (3) of rule 5 of the Indian Forest Service (Recruit- 
ment) Rules, 1966, before it was deleted vide the Depart- 
ment of Personnel’s Notification No- 3/14/72-AISUV) dated 
the 22nd September, 1973 provided that no married woman 
shall be entitled ns of right to be appointed to the Service 
and, where a woman candidate appointed to the Service subse- 
quently marries, the Central Government might, if the main- 
tenance of cffcciency so requires, call upon her to resign 
from service. Similar provision discriminating against marri- 
ed women also existed in the Indian Forest Service (Appoint- 
ment by Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1967, vide 
sub-regulation (2) of regulation 10 thereof. 

With the amendment of the Indian Forest Service (Recruit- 
ment) Rules, 1966, as above, the provision in the Indian Forest 
(Appointment by Competitive Examination) Regula- 
tions, 1967 has become reduptant and unnecessary inasmuch 
as no provision in these Regulations which have been made 
in exercise of the powers vested under the Indian Forest 
Service (Recruitment) Rules, 1967, can be inconsistent with 
the provisions of the Recruitment Rules. It is, therefore, 
necessary to delete sub-regulation (2) of regulation 10 In the 
Indian Forest Service (Appointment by Competitive Examina- 
tion) Regulations, 1967 with effect from the 6th October, 
1973 i.e. the date of publication in the Gazette of India of 
the Notification amending the Indian Forest Service (Recruit- 
ment) Rules, 1966. The Indian Forest Service (Appointment 
by Competitive Examination) Regulations, 1967 are accord- 
ingly being amended with effect from 6th October, 1973, 

Since the amendment is in the nature of liberalising the 
existing provisions, and to conform to the provisions in the 
Indian Forest Service (Recruitment) Rules, 1966 no person 
governed by the Indian Forest Service (Appointment by Com- 
petitive Examination) Regulations, 1967 is likely to be affect- 
ed adversely by the Notification being given retrospective 
effect.

GSR 2504. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
roviso to sub-section (1) of section 594 of the Eompames 
ret 1956 (1 of 1956) read with the Government of India, 
lepartment of Company Affairs, Notification No. G.S.R. 
43(E) dated the 1 8th October, 1972. and in partial mod’fica- 
ion of the Notification of Government of India, Ministry 
f Finance, (Department of Company Law Administration) 
Jo. S R.O. 3216 dated the 4th October, 1957, (hereinafter

referred to as “the notification"), the Company Law Board 
hereby directs that in the case of M/s, Ataka & Co, Ltd., 
(hereinafter referred to as “the company”) being a foreign 
company, the requirements of clause (a) of sub-section (1) 
of the said Section 594 as modified in thefr application to a 
Foreign Company by the Notification shall apply subject to 
the following further exceptions and modifications, namely :■ — 

2, It shall be deemed to be sufficient compliance with the 
revisions of clause (a) of sub-section (1) of the said section 
94, if in respect of the financial years ended on the 3 1st 
March, 1974, 31st March, 1975 and 31st March, 1976, the 
cpmpany submits to the appropriate Registrars of Com- 
panies in India, in triplicate the following documents : 

(i) Copies of its World Accounts as filed by it with a 

prescribed authority in country of its incorporation 
with its copies duly translated in English ; 

(ii) Copies of statement of receipts and disbursements 

made by (he Indian Branches duly audited by a 


[Pari U— 


Chartered Accountant practising in India and signed 
by the authorised person as defined under section 
592 (1) (d) of the Companies Act, 1956; 

(Hi/ Copies of Statement of Assets and Liabilities of the 
Company's branch in India duly authenticated as 
stated in item (ii) above ; and 

(iv) A certificate from two directors and all the authorised 
persons (as defined under section 592(l)(d) of Com- 
panies Act, 1956) to the effect that the company 
has not carried on or done any business in India 
during the period of respective financial year. 

By order of the Company Law Board.

New Dilhi, the 15th Ssptombc’', 1975 

G-S-R. 2505. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby makes 
the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Senior Gzslctnci Operator in the Department of Company Affairs 
namely 

1 . Short title and commencement : — (1) These r les may be called the Department of Company Affairs (Senior Gestetner Opera- 
tor) Recruitment Rules, 1975. 

(2) These rules shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of post, its classification and scale of pay: —The name and number of the post, its classification and the scale of pay 
attached thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the said Schedule. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications, etc. : — The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and other matter, 
relating to the said post shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the said Schedule. 

4. Disqualifications— No person- 

fa) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, shall be eligible for appointmentt to 
said post: 


Provided that the Central Government may, if statisfled that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to such 
person and the other party to the marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this rule. 

5. Power to relax : — Wohrc the Central Government Is of opinion that it is necessary or expeiient so to do, it may, by order for 
reasons to be recorded in writing, relax any of the provisions of these rules in respect of any class or category of per* ms. 

6 Savings. Nothing In these rules shall affect reservations and other concessions required to be provided for Scheduled Castes 

and Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government from time 
to time in this regard.

II. “Inclusion in Ihc list not to confer right to appoint-

list referred to in i ulc 9 confers no right to appoint-

such inquiry as may be considered necessary, that 
the candidate, having regard to hi s ehacacter and

Provided that where a candidate whose name is 
included in the list referred to in rule 9 is not

shall be recorded by the Central Government”.

sion of India, London.

New Delhi.

Delhi.

Affaiis, Ministry of Finance, New Delhi,

CF.NTRA1. EXCISE 

G.S.R. 2511, — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 37 of the Central Excises and Salt Act, 1944 (I of 
1944), the Central Government hereby makes the followina 
titles further to amend the Central Excise Rules. 1944. 
namely:— 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Central Excise (14th 
Amendment) Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Central Excise Rides, 1944, 

(a) for rule 51, the following rule shall be substituted. 

namely:— 

"51. packing and weighment of goods-Unless specially 
exempted by the Collector by order in writting. 
every manufacturer shall as soon as practicable, 
after any excisable goods arc packed and weighed 
in the factory or otherwise made ready for removal 
from the factory- 

tit mark on cnch wholesale package, in a clearly legi- 
ble manner: — 

(a) batch No. (lot No.) if any, to which the goods 
pertain; 

(b) a running serial No. which will commence from the 

1st of January of every year; 

(c) the number of retail packages contained in each 

wholesale package, and the quantity of goods con- 
tained in each retail package; 

(d) a distinguishing letter or letters, or a word or words 

or a combination thereof, denoting the kind and 
* quality of the goods; 

Provided that where there is any doubt or difficulty regarding 
the manner of marking anv of the particulars at (a) to (d) 
on a wholesale packuge, the same may be done in the manner 
approved by the concerned Assistant Collector of Central 
Excise; 

Provided further that the approval under the first proviso 
shall be subject to the modification, if any, that may he 
made by the collector: 

Provided also that where the goods cannot, by reason of 
their nature or for any special reason, be enclosed in packages, 
they shall themselves he marked and distinguished or. 
if (hey cannot be marked, shall be otherwise distinguished 
in such manner as the Collector may require. 

fii) deposit the goods in an approved bonded store 
room unless they are intended to the cleared on 
payment of duty immediately after completion of 
manufacture: 

Provided that where the goods, are to be converted into 
some other form of manufactured product and cannot, there- 
fore. or for any other sufficient reason, be deposited in a 
store-room, they shall be disposed of in such manner as the 
Collector may require. 

Explanation.— For the purpose of this rule, in case of a 
doubt regarding what is a wholesale package or, as the case 
may be, a retail package, the same shall be determined by 
the Assistant Collector of Central Excise having regard to 
the normal trade practice for clearing such goods”; 

(b) In Appendix 1(11) Specimen Forms, — 

(i) in Form E.B. 4 (Central Excise Series No. 36)— 
for the existing column 3. the following shall he 
substituted, namely: — 


“No. of packages Batch No. or Serial Numbers of 
Lot No. Packages 

3(a) 3(b) 3(c)" 



2824 THE GAZE El E OF INDIA : OCTOBER 1 1, 1975/ASVINA 19, 1897 [Pari II- - 


(2) in Form (j.P I. and G.P. 2 (Central Excise Scries 
Nos 65A and 65AA) — 

(a) for the owtds ‘Time of removal;" the woids “Time 
of Removal, in figures and words:" shall be substi- 
tuted; 

(b) in the Table, in the fourth column, in the heading 

for the words "Identification Marks”, the woids 
“Identification Matks and Serial number of the 
goods” shall be substituted.

G.S.R. 2512, — In exercise of the powers conferred by sec- 
tion 37 of the Central Excises and Salt Act, 1944 (1 of 1944) 
the Central Government hereby makes the following 

rules further to amend the Central Excise Rules, 1944 
namely:— 

1.(1) These rules may be called the Central Excise (15th 
Amendment) Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into foicc on the date of their 
publication in Ihe official Gazette. 


2. In rule 56A of the Central Excise Rules, 1944: — • 

(a) in sub-rule (2), in sub-clause (a) of clause (ii) of 

the first proviso, the words ‘or sub-item' shall bo 
omitted; 

(b) Liftei sub-rule (2). the following sub-rule shall be 
inserted, namely:— 

“(2A) Pending the grant of permission by the Collector 
on an application made under sub-rule (2), a manu- 
facturer may. subject to this rule and the condi- 
tions, if any, that may be imposed by the Collector, 
receive material or component parts or finished 
product (like asbestos cement) referred to in that 
sub-rule in his factory and may provisionally take 
credit in respect of the duty paid on such raw 
material 01 component parts of finished products, 
and may also provisionally utilise such credit for 
the purpose for which it would have been permissi- 
ble to utilise the said credit had (he permission to 
nvail of the procedure under this rule been already 
granted on the date of utilising such credit: 

Ptovidcd that if the permission to avail of the pro- 
ccduic under this rule, is eventually refused, duty 
due on the goods cleared on the strength of the 
ptovisional Credit teferred to above, shall be 
paid forthwith (and without any demand being 
made by the proper officer in this behalf) by ad- 
justment in the account current maintained bv 
the manufacturer in terms of rule 9 or rule 173-G, 
as the case may be, or in cash."

New Delhi, the 11th October, 1975 
CENTRAL EXCISE 

G.S.R. 2S13. — In exercise of the powers conferred by rule 
I74A of the Cential Excise Rules, 1944, the Central Govern- 
ment being satisfied that it is necessary and expedient in 
the public interests so to do, hereby exempts from the 
operation of rule 174 of the said rules “other bids”, falling 
under sub-item II (3)(ii) of Item No. 4 of the First Schedule 
to the Central Excises and Salt Act, 1944 (I of 1944), so 
long ns such biris remain exempt from the whole of the 
duty of excise leviable thereon.

DEPARTMENT OF PARLIAMENTARY AFFAIRS 

New Delhi, the 24th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2515. — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
the proviso to article 309, of the Constitution and of all other 
powers enabling him in this behalf, the President hereby 
makes the following rules further to amend the Department 
of Parliamentary Affairs (Recruitment and Conditions of 
Service) Rules, 1963, namely : — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Department of Parlia- 
mentary Affairs (Recruitment and Conditions of Service) Third 
Amendment Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall be deemed to have come into force on the 
17th day of April, 1974. 

2. In the Department of Parliamentary Affairs (Recruit- 
ment and Conditions of Service) Rules, 1963, in Schedule, IV, 
the note below Item No. 4 shall be omitted. 

EXPLANATORY MEMORANDUM 

After the revision of pay scales consequent on the ac- 
ceptance of the recommendations made by the Third Pay 
Commission and the reduction of eligibility period for pro- 
motion from 6 years to 3 years, the need for such a restric- 
tive clause viz., Note below item No, 4 of the Schedule IV 
of the Department of Parliamentary Affairs (Recruitment and 
Conditions of Service) Rules, 1963 no longer exists. It 
has accordingly been decided to omit the said note from the 
17th April, 1974, which is the date on which the first batch 
of Grade I Officers became eligible for promotion to Steno- 
grapher Selection Grade of this Department, Accordingly, 
the Department of Parliamentary Affairs (Recruitment and 
Conditions of Service) Third Amendment Rules, 1975 are 
being given retrospective effect from the 17th April, 1974. 
It is certified that the interests of no one will be prejudically 
affected by retrospective effect being given to the notification. 

[No. F. l(7)/64-Admn.[ 
C. B. L. SAXENA, Under Secy.

MINISTRY OF AGRICULTURE AND IRRIGATION 
(Department of Agriculture) 

New Delhi, the 2nd September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2518 . — In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of tho Constitution and in supersession of the Depart- 
ment of Agriculture (Agricultural Commissioner) Recruitment Rules, 1968 the President hereby makes the following rules regulating the 
method of recruitment to the post of Agricultural Commissioner in the Ministry of Agriculture and Irrigation (Department of Agricul- 
ture), namely : — 

1, Short title and commencement; — (1) These rules may be called the Department of Agriculture (Agricultural Commissioner) 
Recruitment Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number of posts, its classification and scale of pay ’ The number of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay 
attached thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed to these rules. 



SEC. 3(i)] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA: OCTOBER 11, 1975/ASVINA 19, 1897 2855 


3. Methods of r rcruitment, age limit, qualifications, etc.: — The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and other 
matters relating to the said post, shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 

4. Disqualifications : — 

No person — 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person; 
shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to such 
person and the other party to the marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this rule, 

5. Power to relax :— Whore the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by order, for 
reasons to be recorded in writing and in consultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the provisions of these 
rules with respect to any class or category of persons. 

6. Savings Nothing in those rules shall affect reservations and other concessions required to be provided for the Scheduled Castes, 
the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government from time 
to time In this regard.

New Delhi, the 17th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2517 — In accordance with clause (a) of Rule 3 
of the Rules for the administration of the Indian People's 
Famine Trust published under the late Department of Revenue 
and Agriculture No. 1616-F dated 25th, luly, 1900 as amended 
from time to time, the Central Govt, is pleased to appoint 
Secretary to the Government of India Ministry of Agriculture 
& Irrigation (Department of Agriculture) to be a member 
of the Board of Management, Indian People’s Famine Trust 
in place of Secretary to the Government of lndin, (Depart- 
ment of Food).

(Department of Irrigation) 

New Delhi, (he 17th September, 1975 

G S R- 2518.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby makes 
the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post ol' Senior Draftsman Grade I in the Office of the Narmada Water 
Disputes Tribunal under the Ministry of Agriculture and Irrigation (Department of Irrigation), namely 

1. Short -title and commencement, — -(1) These rules may be called the Narmada Water Disputes Tribunal (Senior Draftsman 

Grade I) Recruitment Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on (he date of (heir publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Number, Classification and Scale of Pay .—The number of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexe 1 , to these rules. 

3. Method of Recruitment. — Age Limits and other Qualifications. — •The method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications and other 
matters relating to the said post shall be as specified in columns 5 to 1 3 of the said Schedule. 

4. Disqualification.— No person,-- 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who having a spouse living has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, 
shall be eligible for appointment to the said post; 

Provided that the Central Government may if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to such 
person and the other party to the marriage and that there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this 
rule, 


5. Power to relax.— Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may, by oraer, for 
reasons to be recorded in writing, relax anv of the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or category of person. 

6. Saving,— Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations and other concessions required to be provided for the Scheduled Caste, 
the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the Orders issued by the Cental Government from time 
to time in this regard.

Sec. 3(i)] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA; OCTOBER 11, 1975/ASVINA 19, 1897 


MINISTRY OF EDUCATION AND SOCIAL WELFARE 
New Delh>, the 20th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2519. — Tn exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby makes 
the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Store Keeper in the Central Hindi Directorate in the Ministry of 
Education & Social Welfare, namely ; — 

I , Short Title and commencement: — (I) These rules may be calico theCentral Hindi Directorate (Store Keeper) Recruitment Rules, 


(l) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the official Gazette. 

2 Number of posts, Classification and Scale of Pay The number of the said posts, classification and the scale of pay attached 
thereto shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed hereto. 

3. Method of Recruitment, Age limit and qualifications etc;— The method of recruitment of the said post, age limit educational 
qualification and other matters connceted therewith shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 

4. Disqualification:— No person, — 


(a) Who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person havirig a spouse living, 

or 

(b) Who having a spouse living has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, 
shall be eligible for appointment to the said post; 

Provided that the Central Government may, if Satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to such 
person and the other party to the marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this rule, 

5. Power to Relax;— Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may by order for rea- 
sons to be recorded In writing, relax any of the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or category of persons. 

6. Saving: — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations and other concessions required to be provided for Scheduled Castes, 
Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government from time to 
time in this regard.

Sec. 3(i)] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA: OCTOBER 11, 1975/ASVINA 19, 1897 2861 


G.S.R. 2520,— la exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, ihe President hereby makes 
the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Addressograph Operator in the Central Hindi Directorate in the 
Ministry of Education & Social Welfare, namely : — 

1. Short title and commencement. — (1) These rules may be called the Central Hindi Directorate (Addressograph Operator) Recruit- 
ment Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the official Gazette. 

2. Number, Classification and scale of pay.- -The number of the said post, its classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in Columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed hereto. 

3. Method of recruitment, age limit and qualifications etc. —The method of recruitment to the said post, ago limit educational 
qualifications and other matteis connected therowith shall be as specified in Columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 

4. Disqualification. —No person, (a) who has ontered into or conti acted a marriage with a person having a spouse living, or 

(b) who, having a spouso living, has entered into or contracted ft marriage with any person, shall bo eligible for appointment to the 
said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such marriage is permissible under the personal law applicable to 
such person and the other party to tire marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation 
of this rule. 

5. Powet to Relax. —Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may by order and 
for reason to be recordod in writing relax any of the provisions of these rules with respect to any class or category of persons. 

6. Saving. —Nothing in these rules shall affect reservation, and other concessions required to be provided for the Scheduled Castes, 
the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government from time 
to time in this regard .

[Part II— 

(Department of Education) 

New Delhi, the 29th September, 1975 

G.S.R, 2521. — -In exercise of the powers conferred by 
sub-section (1), read with clause (d) of sub-section (2) of 
section 25, of the University Grants Commission Act, 1956 
(3 of 1956), the Central Government hereby makes the 
following rules further to amend the University Grants Com- 
mission (Terms and Conditions of Service of Employees) 
Rules, 1958, namely : — 

(1) These rules may be called the University Grants 
Commission (Terms and Conditions of Service of 
Employees) Second amendment) Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the official Gazette. 

2. In the University Grants Commission (Terms and Con- 
ditions of Service of Employees) Rules, 1958, for sub-rule 
(l> of rule 4, the following shall be substituted, namely : — 

“(1) Recruitment to all posts under the Commission shall 

be made — 

(a) by direct recruitment by advertisement or through 
the Employment Exchange or through the Institute 
of Secretariat Training and Management, Depart- 
ment <pf Personnel, or, where the Commission of 
the view that candidates of exceptional merit have 
to be located for specialised posts, by contact; 

(b) promotion ; or 

(c) by transfer from other Central or State Government 
of Semi-Government Offices or universities or other 
organisations or, where the Commission is of the 
view that it is absolutely necessary so to do, by re- 
employment of superannuated persons belonging to 
Central or State Government or Seml-Govcmment 
Offices or universities or other organisations." 

[No. F. 16-50/75-leg-unit] 
ANIL BORDIA, Director.

MINISTRY OF SHIPPING AND TRANSPORT 

(Transport Wing) 

Now Delhi, the 10th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2522.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President hereby 
makes the following rules regulating the method of recruitment to the post of Assistant Marine Engineer (Floafi/a) in the Andaman, Lak- 
shdweep Harbour Works, Ministry of Shipping and Transport, namely :— 

1. Short Title and Commencement: — (1) These rules may be called the Andaman, Lakshdweep Harbour Works Assistant Marine 
Engineer (Floatila) Recruitment Rules, 197?, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the official gazette. 

2. Number of posts, classification and scale of pay: — The number of the said posts, classification and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified In columns 2 to 4 of the Schedule annexed hereto. 

3. Method of recruitment, age-limit and qualifications etc : — The method of recruitment, age-iimit, qualifications and other matters 
relating to the said post shall be as specified in columns 5 to 13 of the Schedule aforesaid. 

4. Liability to serve in Defence Service or post connected with the Defence of India : — Any person appointed to the post specified 
in the said Schedule, shall, if so required be liable to serve in any Defence Service or post connected with the Defence of India for a period 
of not less than four years including the period spent on training, if any ; 

Provided that such person, 

(a) shall not be required to serve as aforesaid after the expiry of ten years from the date of appointment; 

(b) shall not ordinarily be required to serve as aforesaid after attaining the age of forty years. 



2865 


Sec. 3(i)] THE GAZETTE OF INDIA: OCTOBER 11, 1975/ASVINA 19, 1897 


5. Disqualifications No person— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living; or 

(b) who having a spouse living has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person; 
shall be eligible for appointment to the said post : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if specified that such, a marriage is admissible under the personal law applicable to such 
person and the other party to the marriage and there arc other grounds for so doing exempt any person from the operation of this rule. 

6. Power to relax : — Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may by order, for rea 
sons to be recorded in writing and in consultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the provisions of these rules 
with respect to any class or category of persons. 

7. Saving : — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations and other concessions required to be provided to the candidates belonging 
to the Scheduled Castes, the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central 
Government from time to time in this regard.

MINISTRY OF WORKS AND HOUSING 
(Works Division) 

New Delhi, the 27th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2523.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President here- 
by makes the following rules further to amend the Central 
Public Works Department, Architectural Staff (Gazetted) Re- 
cruitment Rules, 1962, namely 

1. Short title and Commencement. — (1) These rules may be 
called the Central Public Works Department, Architectural 
Staff (Gazetted) Recruitment (Amendment) Rules, 1975, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub- 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. Tn the Schedule to the Central Public Works Department 
Architectural Staff (Gazetted) Recruitment Rules, 1962, 
against the post of Chief Architect, (a) for the enry in 
column 3, the following entry shall be substituted namely: — 

“Rs. 2200— 125/2—2500— EB— 125/2— 2750 (Revised)”; 

(b) for the entry in column 10, the following entry shall 
be substituted, namely : — 

“Five years' regular service in the grade of Senior Ar- 
chitect.”

MINISTRY OF SUPPLY & REHABILITATION 
(Department of Rehabilitation) 

New Delhi, the 16th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2524.— In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
proviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President here- 
by makes the following rules to amend the DandakarHnaya 
Project (Class-Ill posts) Recruitment Rules, 1973, namely : — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Dandakaranaya Pro- 

ject I Class-Ill posts) Recruitment (Amendment) 
Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the schedule to the Dandakaranaya Project (Class-Ill 
posts) Recruitment Rules, 1973, in the entries relating to 
the post of Gestetnor Operator, in the entry in column f for 
the expression, "Gestetnor Operator" the expression "Gestet- 
nor Operator/Gestetnor Operator-cum-Record keeper", shall 
be substituted.

MINISTRY OF COMMUNICATIONS 
(P&T Board) 

New Delhi, the 24th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2525. — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 21 of the Indian Post Office Act, 1898 (6 of 1898), 
(he Central Government hereby makes the following rules 
further to amend the Indian Post Office Rules, 1933, name- 
l y : — 

(1 ) These rules may be called the Indian Post Office 
(Sixth Amendment) Rules 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their 
publication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Indian Post Office Rules, 1933, in rule 183, foi 
clause (b), the following clause shall be substituted, name- 
ly : — - 

(b) The Vice-Chancellor and Registrar of 0 University 
established by or under a Central, Provincial or 
State Act, provided that the articles posted by them 
relate solely to the public business of the Univer- 
sity”.

MlNfSTRV OF INFORMATION AND BROADCASTING 
New Delhi, the 3rd September, 1975 

G, S. R. 2526. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the provisio to article 309 of the Constitution the President hereby 
makes the following rules for regulating the method of recruitment to Class I and Class IT posts in the Board of Film Censors, under 
the Ministry of Information and Broadcasting, namely ; — 

1. Short title and Commencement: — (1) These rules may be called the Board of film Censors (Class 1 and Class II posts) Recruit- 
ment Rules, 1975, 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication In the Official Gazette. 

2. Application : — These rules shall apply to the poss specified in Column 1 of the Schedule annexed lo these rules. 

3. Number classification and scale of pay: — The number of the said posts, their classificalion and the scale of pay attached thereto 
shall be as specified in columns 2 to 4 of ihe said Schedule. 

4. Method of recruitment, age limit, qualifications etc: — The method of recruitment lo the said posts, 
other matters connected therewith shall be as specified in columns 5 to 1 3 of the aforesaid Schedule : — 

5. Probation : — All persons appointed whether by direct recruitment or otherwise to any of the posts 
aforesaid Schedule shall be on probation for Ihe period specified against such post in column 9 thereof ; 

Provided that the period of probation may be extended by the appointing authority for such further 
years, on year to year basis, as it deems fit. 

6. Disqualification No person,— 

(a) who has entered into or contracted a marriage with a person having a spouse living; or 

(b) who, having a spouse living, has entered into or contracted a marriage with any person, 
shall be eligible for appointment to any of the said posts : 

Provided that the Central Government may, if satisfied that such’marriagc is permissible under the Personal Law applicable to such 
person and the other party to the marriage and there are other grounds for so doing, exempt any person from the operation of this rule. 

7. Power to Relax : — Where the Central Government is of opinion that it is necessary or expedient so to do, it may by older, 
for reasons to be recorded in writing and in consultation with the Union Public Service Commission, relax any of the provisions of 
these rules with respect to any class or category of persons. 


age limit, qualifications and 
specified in column 1 of the

8 Saving : — Nothing in these rules shall affect reservations and othei concessions required to be provided for the schedule Castes 
the Scheduled Tribes and other special categories of persons in accordance with the orders issued by the Central Government from time 
to time in this regard. 

9. Repeal and saving -The Central Board of Film Censors (Class I and Class II posts) Recruitment Rules, 1963 is hereby repealed 

Provided lhai any order made or action taken under the rules so repealed shall be deemed to have been made or taken under the 
corresponding piovisions of these rules.

New Delhi, the 16th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2527. — Jn exercise of the powers conferred by 
the proviso to article 309 of the Consttiution, the President 
hereby makes the folio wins rules further to amend the Films 
Division (Recruitment to Class III and Class IV posts) rules 
1959, namely : — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Films Division (Rec- 
ruitment to Class III and Class IV posts) Second Amendment 
Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub- 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2, In the Schedule to the Films Division (Recruitment to 
Class III and Class IV posts) Rules, 1959, 

(a) against serial number 7 relating to Background Artist, — 

(i) in column 5 for the existing entry “100 per cent”, 
the entry “Nil” shall he substituted ; 

(ii) in column 6 for the existing entry “NIL”, the entry 
“100 per cent"’ shall be substituted ; 

(iii) In column 10, under the heading “Essential” lor 
item (i) and the entry against it, the following item 
and entry shall be substituted, namely : — 

"(i) Degree or Diploma in Fine Arts or Commercial 
Arts from a recognised University.”; 

(iv) in column 11, for tbe existing entry the following 
entry shall be substituted namely : — 

“Will not apply in the case of permanent, or quasi- 
permanent persons.” ; 

(v) in column 12, for the existing entry the following 
entry shall be substituted, namely : — 

“Promotion. — From among Story Illustrator, Assistant 
Layout Artist and Assistant Background Artist with 
three years regular service in their respective 
grades”. 

(b) against serial numbers 2, 3, 4, 5 and 6 relating to 
Artist Grade 11, Artist Grade I, Story Illustrator, Assistant 
Layout Artist and Assistant Background Artist respectively, in 
column 10, for item (ii) and the entry against it under 
heading “Essential", the following item and entry shall be 
substituted, namely ; — ■ 

(ii) Degree or Diploma in Fine Arts or Commercial 
Arts from a recognised Institute."

MINISTRY OF RAILWAYS 
(Railway Board) 

New Delhi, the 19th September, 1975 

G.S.R. 2528. — In exercise of the powers conferred by 
section 47 of the Indian Railways Act, 1890 (9 of 1890), the 
Central Government hereby makes the following rules further 
to amend the Railways Red Tariff Rules, 1960, namely : — 

1. (1) These rules shall be called the Railways Red Tariff 
(5th Amendment) Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publi- 
cation in the Official Gazette. 


2. In the Railways Red Tariff Rujes, 1960, in Chapter I, 
Table I, under Class 6, under the heading ‘Ammunition’, under 
Division 2, against the item ‘Detonating fuze’ in column 1, 
for the existing entries in column 7, the following shall be 
substituted, namely : — 

“May be permitted for carriage in the rear brake-van of 
Passenger, Mixed or Parcels train subject to the following 
conditions 

(a) one reel containing 305 metres of Detonating fuze 
or 500 metres of Detonating fuze (T-Cord) is packed 
in a wooden case lined with corrugated paper board ; 

83 GI/75— 10 


(b) not more than one package of Detonating fuze or 
Detonating fuze (I-Cord), packed as above, ie per- 
mitted to be carried by any one train ; and 

(c) no detonators are carried in the same carriage.” 

[No, 74 TG. IV/21/8] 
A, L, GUPTA, Secy.

New Delhi, the 26th September, 1975 

G.S K, 2530. — In exercise of the powers conferred by the 
roviso to article 309 of the Constitution, the President 
ereby makes the following rules further to amend the Labour 
Officers (Central Pool) Recruitment and Conditions of Ser- 
vice Rules, 1951, namely : — 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Labour Officers 
(Central Pool) Recruitment and Conditions of Service 
(Amendment) Rules, 1975. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub- 
lication in the Official Gazette. 


2. In the Labour Officers (Central Pool) Recruitment 
and Conditions of Service Rules, 1951 — 

(1) for sub-rule (2) of rule 4, the following sub-rule 
shall be substituted, namely : — 


11, 1975/ASVJNA 19, 1897 [Part II— Sec. 3(i)] 


“(2) Recruitment to posts in the Selection Grade 
shall be made by promotion by selection on 
the recommendations of a duly constituted 
Departmental Promotion Committee from 
among Labour Officers, who have rendered 
at least eight years’ service in the Pool, on 
the Pool basis and not Ministrywise. The 
Labour Officers so promoted will draw the 
Selection Grade pay as personal to them 
irrespective of their place of posting.”; 

(2) for rule 5, the following rule shall be substituted 

namely : — 

“5. Qualifications — Essential — 

(i) A Bachelor’s Degree in Arts of a recognised 
University or equivalent with Economics or 
Commerce or Sociology as one of the sub- 
jects; 

(ii) A post-graduate Degree or Diploma in Social 
Work or Labour Welfare or Industrial Rela- 
tions or Personnel Management or in any 
other allied subject, of a recognised Univer- 
sity or equivalent; 

(iii) Two years’ experience of Labour Welfare 
Work, Industrial Relations or Personnel Mana- 
gement in an organisation employing sub- 
stantial Labour force. 

Desirable : 

Degree in Law of a recognised University,” 

TF. No. A-12018/4/7 4-PLOl
